text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">根据我校202</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">6</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">年硕士研究生招生计划及一志愿复试录取基本情况，我校部分学科专业（方向）可接受考生调剂。现将我校调剂工作的有关事项通知如下，欢迎全国各地优秀考生积极申请我校调剂：</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><strong><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">一、调剂基本要求</span></strong></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（一）须符合我校《202</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">6</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">年硕士研究生招生简章及招生专业目录》公布的调入专业报考条件（包括各专业备注栏中的要求）。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（二）初试成绩符合第一志愿报考专业在A区的初试成绩基本要求。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（三）调入专业与第一志愿报考专业相同或相近。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（四）初试科目与调入专业初试科目相同或相近，其中初试全国统一命题科目与调入专业全国统一命题科目原则上应相同。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（五）第一志愿报考法律硕士（非法学）专业的考生不得调入其他专业，其他专业的考生也不得调入该专业。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（六）第一志愿报考照顾专业（指体育学及体育硕士，工学照顾专业）的考生若调剂出本类照顾专业，其初试成绩必须达到调入地区该照顾专业所在学科门类（类别）的全国初试成绩基本要求。第一志愿报考非照顾专业的考生若调入照顾专业，其初试成绩必须符合调入地区对应的非照顾专业学科门类（类别）的全国初试成绩基本要求。体育学与体育硕士，工学照顾专业之间调剂按照顾专业内部调剂政策执行。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（七）报考临床医学类专业学位硕士研究生的考生可按相关政策调剂到其他专业，报考其他专业的考生不可调剂到临床医学类专业学位。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（八）我校调剂均需通过研招网全国硕士研究生招生考试网上调剂系统（以下简称调剂系统）进行调剂，调剂考生须符合各培养单位制定的其他各项调剂要求，具体可见研招网调剂系统中各专业“调剂特别要求”及各培养单位网站。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">二、调剂程序</strong></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（一）各相关培养单位根据学科需要以及招生计划等情况，在学校调剂要求的基础上制定本单位相关专业的调剂要求，并成立硕士研究生招生复试调剂考生选拔小组。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（二）</span><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">我校调剂系统开放时间为：4月8日00:00-12:00</strong><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">。申</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">请调剂的考生请及时在调剂系统中填报志愿。</span><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">我校调剂志愿锁定时间为</strong><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">24</strong><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">小时</strong><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">，锁定时间到达后，考生可继续填报其他志愿。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（三）</span><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">我校选拔调剂考生的时间为：4月8日12:00以后。</strong><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">各相关培养单位依据考生综合素质情况等因素进行审核筛选，择优遴选进入复试的考生。我校将在调剂系统中发送复试通知，调剂考生须在6小时内进行确认（按教育部调剂系统要求，若时间跨度在22点至第二天8点的，则此段时间不计入6小时以内），不按时确认的，将取消通知。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（四）各调剂专业的综合成绩计算方式、复试排名办法以及录取办法，请考生务必于申请调剂前查阅各相关培养单位网站。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（五）复试要求请参看</span><a href="https://yjsy.ncu.edu.cn/info/1012/47216.htm" target="_self"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#000000; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; text-underline:none; text-decoration:none; background:#FFFFFF">《南昌大学20</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#000000; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; text-underline:none; text-decoration:none; background:#FFFFFF">26</span><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#000000; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; text-underline:none; text-decoration:none; background:#FFFFFF">年硕士研究生复试通知》</span></a><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><strong style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">三、其他注意事项</strong></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（一）考生在申请调剂前，应充分了解我校（含各培养单位）的调剂工作办法，以及相关专业不同学习方式（全日制和非全日制）在招生、培养、奖助、就业等方面的政策区别。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（二）调剂考生除需满足我校调剂基本要求以外，还应仔细核对是否符合各相关培养单位提出的接受调剂专业的报考条件，且保证提交的材料真实有效。对不符合我校调剂条件的考生，一经查实，立即取消其复试资格。若因不符合我校调剂要求或弄虚作假而造成无法复试或者无法通过上级招考部门录取检查的，由考生自行负责。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（三）请各位调剂考生务必认真阅读学校及各相关培养单位调剂工作办法的要求，慎重填报调剂志愿，以免造成不符合我校调剂条件而耽误调剂时间。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（四）我校调剂复试采取线上线下相结合的方式，各调剂考生应提前查看所报学院和专业的调剂细则，了解报到及复试的时间、地点，合理规划行程，按时到达指定地点参加复试，未按时参加复试者，其复试资格无效。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt; font-family:仿宋_GB2312; color:#333333; font-variant:normal; text-transform:none; letter-spacing:0.0pt; font-style:normal; background:#FFFFFF">（五）经复试合格的拟录取调剂考生，我校将通过研招网调剂系统发送待录取通知，调剂考生须在6小时内进行确认（按教育部调剂系统要求，若时间跨度在22点至第二天8点的，则此段时间不计入6小时以内），不按时接受通知的，我校将取消其拟录取资格，递补录取其他考生。</span></p><p style="margin-right: 0%; text-align: justify; text-indent: 32.25pt; line-height: 27pt; break-before: auto; break-after: auto; word-break: normal; direction: ltr; background: rgb(255, 255, 255); margin-top: 5px; margin-bottom: 5px;"><span style="font-size:15.0pt;
